Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the membranes object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 510. The moduli space has dimension at most 56 with 1 connected components.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to membranes structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 510/56 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
